L /**
T 3 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java punctuation.definition.comment.java
L  * This class assists in validating arguments. The validation methods are
T 73 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  * based along the following principles:
T 40 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  * <ul>
T 7 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  *   <li>An invalid {@code null} argument causes a {@link NullPointerException}.</li>
T 85 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  *   <li>A non-{@code null} argument causes an {@link IllegalArgumentException}.</li>
T 85 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  *   <li>An invalid index into an array/collection/map/string causes an {@link IndexOutOfBoundsException}.</li>
T 111 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  * </ul>
T 8 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  *
T 2 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  * <p>
T 6 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  * All exceptions messages are
T 30 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  * <a href="https://docs.oracle.com/javase/8/docs/api/java/util/Formatter.html#syntax">format strings</a>
T 105 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  * as defined by the Java platform. For example:
T 48 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  *
T 2 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  * <pre>
T 8 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  * Validate.isTrue(i &gt; 0, "The value must be greater than zero: %d", i);
T 75 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  * Validate.notNull(surname, "The surname must not be %s", null);
T 65 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  * </pre>
T 9 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  *
T 2 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  * <p>
T 6 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  * #ThreadSafe#
T 15 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  * </p>
T 7 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  *
T 2 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  * @see String#format(String, Object...)
T 3 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
T 7 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java keyword.other.documentation.javadoc.java
T 40 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  * @since 2.0
T 3 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
T 9 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java keyword.other.documentation.javadoc.java
T 13 source.java comment.block.javadoc.java
L  */
